PREFACE 


This brochure contains an index to the Gazette of India for 
the month of July, 1956. Notifications relating to Acts, Ordiy 
.nances and Regulations (Part II, Section i); Bills and Reports of 

3 ia Committees on Bills (Part II, Section 2); and Stamtor- 
es and Orders (Part II, Sections 3 and 4) have been indexed 
austively. As regards other Notifications (Part I, Sections i 
and 3, Part III, Section i) only items of importance from 
Members’ view point have been indexed. References to the 
P^rts and Sections of Gazette along with the page numbers have 
been given in the index for facility of reference. 


New Delhi, 

Tht i8iA Deumberi 1956. 


M. N. KAUL, 
Secretary.
